USD Hedged","positions":26,"fundVestShares":0,"fundVestValue":0,"nonFundVestShares":411315.13,"nonFundVestValue":4260813.41,"pershingEtfShares":0,"pershingEtfValue":0,"fundVestEtfShares":0,"fundVestEtfValue":0,"totalQuantity":411315.13,"totalValue":4260813.41,"unitValue":10.36}]}</script><script>
const D=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('reportData').textContent);const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);const money=v=>v==null?'':new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US',{style:'currency',currency:'USD',maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(v);const qty=v=>v==null?'':new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:2}).format(v);const d=s=>s?new Date(s+'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US'):'';const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
const pages=[['dashboard','Home'],['clientdesk','Client Desk'],['clientinfo','All Client Data'],['reminders','Update Reminders'],['pnl','Price Movement'],['clients','Accounts'],['funds','Funds'],['trades','Trade History'],['positions','Positions'],['tasks','Tasks'],['meetings','Meetings'],['notes','Notes'],['import','Data Controls']];let state={page:'dashboard',q:'',account:'',fund:'',from:'',to:'',type:'',profileQ:'',profileKey:''};
const load=k=>JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'[]'),save=(k,v)=>localStorage.setItem(k,JSON.stringify(v));
function nav(){ $('#nav').innerHTML=pages.map(([id,n])=>'<button data-page="'+id+'" class="'+(state.page===id?'active':'')+'">'+n+'</button>').join('');document.querySelectorAll('[data-page]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{state.page=b.dataset.page;render()})}
function view(id,html){return '<section class="view '+(state.page===id?'active':'')+'" id="'+id+'">'+html+'</section>'}
function filteredTrades(){return D.trades.filter(t=>(!state.q||JSON.stringify(t).toLowerCase().includes(state.q))&&(!state.account||t.account===state.account)&&(!state.fund||t.cusip===state.fund)&&(!state.type||t.transType===state.type)&&(!state.from||t.tradeDate>=state.from)&&(!state.to||t.tradeDate<=state.to))}
function accountRows(){const m={};D.trades.forEach(t=>{const a=m[t.account]??={account:t.account,sponsor:t.sponsor,branch:t.branchCode,adviser:t.advisorName||t.advisorCode,trades:0,buy:0,sell:0,last:''};a.trades++;if(t.transType==='Buy')a.buy+=t.principal||0;else a.sell+=t.principal||0;if(t.tradeDate>a.last)a.last=t.tradeDate});return Object.values(m).filter(a=>!state.q||JSON.stringify(a).toLowerCase().includes(state.q))}
function fundRows(){const m={};D.positions.forEach(p=>{const f=m[p.cusip]??={cusip:p.cusip,name:p.fundName,style:p.style,quantity:0,value:0,positions:0,unit:p.unitValue};f.quantity+=p.totalQuantity||0;f.value+=p.totalValue||0;f.positions+=p.positions||0});return Object.values(m).sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value).filter(f=>!state.q||JSON.stringify(f).toLowerCase().includes(state.q))}
function table(headers,rows){return '<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>'+headers.map(h=>'<th>'+h+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
function dashboard(){const tr=D.trades, buys=tr.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy').reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.principal||0),0),sells=tr.filter(x=>x.transType==='Sell').reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.principal||0),0), top=fundRows().slice(0,8), tasks=load('cpd_tasks').filter(x=>x.status!=='Done');return view('dashboard','<div class="grid">'+[['Portfolio value',money(D.totals.totalValue)],['Accounts',accountRows().length],['Historical trades',tr.length],['Funds / securities',new Set(tr.map(x=>x.cusip)).size],['Buy principal',money(buys)],['Sell principal',money(sells)],['Open tasks',tasks.length],['Report as of',d(D.reportAsOf)]].map(x=>'<div class="card metric"><div class="muted">'+x[0]+'</div><div class="value">'+x[1]+'</div></div>').join('')+'</div><div class="two section"><div class="card"><h2>Largest observed fund exposures</h2>'+top.map(f=>'<div style="margin:13px 0"><div class="kv"><span>'+esc(f.name)+'</span><b>'+money(f.value)+'</b></div><div class="bar"><span style="width:'+Math.max(1,100f.value/(top[0]?.value||1))+'%"></span></div></div>').join('')+'</div><div class="card"><h2>Attention</h2><div class="notice">P&L is not shown because the reports do not supply an approved account-level cost basis, fees, distributions or FX treatment.</div><div class="kpi-list section"><div class="kv"><span>Position rows</span><b>'+D.positions.length+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Trade date range</span><b>'+d('2026-01-05')+' - '+d('2026-12-08')+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Saved notes</span><b>'+load('cpd_notes').length+'</b></div></div></div></div>')}
function profileDirectory(){
const map={};
const ensure=k=>map[k]??=( {key:k,names:new Set(),companies:new Set(),accounts:new Set(),advisers:new Set(),addresses:new Set(),cities:new Set(),states:new Set(),postals:new Set(),funds:new Map(),trades:[]} );
D.trades.forEach(t=>{
const p=ensure(t.account);
p.accounts.add(t.account);p.names.add(t.advisorName);p.companies.add(t.sponsor);p.companies.add(t.branchName);p.companies.add(t.firm);p.advisers.add(t.advisorName||t.advisorCode);p.addresses.add(t.advisorAddress);p.cities.add(t.city);p.states.add(t.state);p.postals.add(t.postalCode);p.funds.set(t.cusip,t.product);p.trades.push(t);
});
return Object.values(map).map(p=>{for(const k of Object.keys(p))if(p[k] instanceof Set)p[k]=[...p[k]].filter(Boolean);return p});
}
function clientDesk(){
const profiles=profileDirectory();
const term=(state.profileQ||'').toLowerCase().trim();
const matches=term?profiles.filter(p=>JSON.stringify(p).toLowerCase().includes(term)):profiles;
const selected=profiles.find(p=>p.key===state.profileKey)||(matches.length===1?matches[0]:null);
const search='<div class="card"><h2>Person or company lookup</h2><p class="muted">Search an account, adviser, company, branch, fund or CUSIP. Select a result to see its complete historical record on one screen.</p><div class="controls"><input id="profileSearch" style="min-width:min(520px,100%)" placeholder="Name, company, account, fund or CUSIP" value="'+esc(state.profileQ||'')+'"><button class="primary" id="profileSearchBtn">Search</button></div></div>';
const results='<div class="section card"><h2>Matching people, companies and accounts ('+matches.length+')</h2><div style="max-height:420px;overflow:auto">'+(matches.length?matches.map(p=>'<div style="padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)"><span class="account-link" data-profile="'+esc(p.key)+'"><b>'+esc(p.key)+'</b></span><div class="muted">'+esc([...p.names,...p.companies].filter(Boolean).slice(0,4).join(' | '))+'</div></div>').join(''):'<div class="empty">No matching record</div>')+'</div></div>';
if(!selected)return view('clientdesk',search+results);
const back='<div class="controls"><button class="primary" id="backToDesk">\u2190 Back to Client Desk</button><span class="pill">Viewing '+esc(selected.key)+'</span></div>';
const ts=selected.trades.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.tradeDate.localeCompare(a.tradeDate));
const mvAll=(function(){const list=ts.map(t=>{const px=movePrices[t.cusip];const tp=t.quantity?t.principal/t.quantity:null;return px&&tp?{...t,tradePrice:tp,currentPrice:px.unitValue,movePct:(px.unitValue-tp)/tp,markedValue:t.quantitypx.unitValue,unrealized:t.quantity(px.unitValue-tp)}:{...t,tradePrice:tp,currentPrice:px?px.unitValue:null,movePct:null,markedValue:null,unrealized:null}});
const priced=list.filter(x=>x.movePct!==null),buys=priced.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy');
const invested=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0),markedBuys=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.markedValue,0);
return {list,priced,invested,markedBuys,pct:invested?(markedBuys-invested)/invested:null,unreal:markedBuys-invested}})();
const moveRows=mvAll.list.map(t=>'<tr><td>'+d(t.tradeDate)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.cusip)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.product)+'</td><td class="'+(t.transType==='Buy'?'buy':'sell')+'">'+t.transType+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(t.quantity)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(t.tradePrice)+'</td><td class="num">'+(t.currentPrice==null?'No price':money(t.currentPrice))+'</td><td class="num">'+(t.movePct==null?'':(t.movePct>=0?'+':'')+(t.movePct100).toFixed(2)+'%')+'</td><td class="num">'+(t.unrealized==null?'':money(t.transType==='Buy'?t.unrealized:-t.unrealized))+'</td></tr>');
const moveCard='<div class="section"><h2>Price movement on this account\u2019s trades</h2><div class="grid"><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Cost of priced buys</div><div class="value">'+money(mvAll.invested)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Marked value</div><div class="value">'+money(mvAll.markedBuys)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Movement %</div><div class="value">'+(mvAll.pct==null?'-':(mvAll.pct>=0?'+':'')+(mvAll.pct100).toFixed(2)+'%')+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Movement amount</div><div class="value">'+money(mvAll.unreal)+'</div></div></div><div class="notice">Price movement, not accounting profit or loss. Based on average price paid versus the 18 Sep 2026 unit value.</div>'+table(['Date','CUSIP','Product','Type','Quantity','Price at trade','Current price','Movement %','Movement amount'],moveRows)+'</div>';
const buy=ts.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy').reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.principal||0),0),sell=ts.filter(x=>x.transType==='Sell').reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.principal||0),0);
const notes=load('cpd_notes').filter(x=>x.account===selected.key),tasks=load('cpd_tasks').filter(x=>x.account===selected.key),meetings=load('cpd_meetings').filter(x=>x.account===selected.key);
const tradeRows=ts.map(t=>'<tr><td>'+d(t.tradeDate)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.cusip)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.product)+'</td><td class="'+(t.transType==='Buy'?'buy':'sell')+'">'+t.transType+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(t.quantity)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(t.principal)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(t.quantity?t.principal/t.quantity:null)+'</td></tr>');
const fundRows=[...selected.funds].map(([c,n])=>{const ps=D.positions.filter(x=>x.cusip===c);return '<tr><td>'+esc(c)+'</td><td>'+esc(n)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(ps[0]?.unitValue)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(ps.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.totalValue||0),0))+'</td></tr>'});
const details='<div class="section"><div class="grid"><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Account</div><div class="value">'+esc(selected.key)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Historical trades</div><div class="value">'+ts.length+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Buy principal</div><div class="value">'+money(buy)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Sell principal</div><div class="value">'+money(sell)+'</div></div></div><div class="two section"><div class="card"><h2>Identity and contact context</h2><div class="kpi-list"><div class="kv"><span>Adviser / person</span><b>'+esc(selected.names.join(', ')||'Not supplied')+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Company / sponsor</span><b>'+esc(selected.companies.join(', '))+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Address</span><b>'+esc([selected.addresses.join(', '),selected.cities.join(', '),selected.states.join(', '),selected.postals.join(', ')].filter(Boolean).join(' | ')||'Not supplied')+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Last historical trade</span><b>'+d(ts[0]?.tradeDate)+'</b></div></div></div><div class="card"><h2>Conversation preparation</h2><div class="notice">Verified end-client names and account-level cost basis are not present in the supplied reports. Adviser and company names below are report data; they must not be relabeled as verified clients.</div><p><b>Saved tasks:</b> '+tasks.length+' &nbsp; <b>Meetings:</b> '+meetings.length+' &nbsp; <b>Notes:</b> '+notes.length+'</p></div></div><div class="section"><h2>Funds and current aggregate context</h2>'+table(['CUSIP','Fund','Latest unit value','Report-wide observed value'],fundRows)+'</div><div class="section"><h2>Complete trade history</h2>'+moveCard+table(['Date','CUSIP','Product','Type','Quantity','Principal','Implied unit price'],tradeRows)+'</div><div class="two section"><div class="card"><h2>Tasks and follow-ups</h2>'+(tasks.length?tasks.map(x=>'<p><b>'+esc(x.status||'')+'</b> '+esc(x.task||'')+' '+esc(x.due||'')+'</p>').join(''):'<p class="muted">No saved tasks.</p>')+'</div><div class="card"><h2>Meetings and notes</h2>'+(meetings.concat(notes).length?meetings.map(x=>'<p><b>'+esc(x.date||'')+'</b> '+esc(x.summary||'')+'</p>').join('')+notes.map(x=>'<p><b>'+esc(x.timestamp||'')+'</b> '+esc(x.note||'')+'</p>').join(''):'<p class="muted">No saved meetings or notes.</p>')+'</div></div></div>';
return view('clientdesk',search+back+details);
}

const TODAY='2026-09-21';
const REPORTDATE=D.reportAsOf;
const movePrices={"900934506":{"unitValue":41.24,"fundName":"Pacific NoS Global EM Equity Active ETF (GEME)","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S69W727":{"unitValue":17.51,"fundName":"Pacific Multi-Asset Acc Plus USD C Hdg","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6AY747":{"unitValue":13.56,"fundName":"Pac Nrth of Sth Glb EM Eq I USD Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6B9261":{"unitValue":13.11,"fundName":"Pac Nrth of Sth Glb EM Eq R1 USD Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G68488116":{"unitValue":10.16,"fundName":"PACIFIC CAP COOLABAH CREDIT ALPHA FUND CLASS S1","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G68488124":{"unitValue":10.88,"fundName":"PACIFIC NORTH SOUTH GLOBAL EMERGING MARKETS EQUITY FD","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S69W586":{"unitValue":15.68,"fundName":"Pacific Multi-Asset Acc Core USD D1 Hdg","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S69W602":{"unitValue":15.14,"fundName":"Pacific Multi-Asset Acc Core $ C Hdg","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S69W693":{"unitValue":19.49,"fundName":"Pacific Multi-Asset Acc Plus USD Ins","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S69W701":{"unitValue":18.1,"fundName":"Pacific Multi-Asset Acc Plus USD D1 Hdg","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6B9394":{"unitValue":10.36,"fundName":"Pacific Coolabah Credit Alp R USD Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6B9402":{"unitValue":10.36,"fundName":"Pacific Coolabah Credit Alp R4 USD Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6B9410":{"unitValue":10.42,"fundName":"Pacific Coolabah Credit Alp I USD Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"},"G6S6AY572":{"unitValue":9.94,"fundName":"Pacific Coolabah Glb Act Crdt Cl R $ Acc","asOf":"2026-09-18"}};
const daysBetween=(a,b)=>Math.round((new Date(b+'T00:00:00')-new Date(a+'T00:00:00'))/86400000);
function accountStats(){
const m={};
D.trades.forEach(t=>{const a=m[t.account]??={account:t.account,sponsor:t.sponsor,branchName:t.branchName,firm:t.firm,adviser:t.advisorName||t.advisorCode,address:t.advisorAddress,city:t.city,state:t.state,postal:t.postalCode,cusipSet:new Set(),trades:0,buy:0,sell:0,first:'',last:''};
a.trades++;a.cusipSet.add(t.cusip);a.buy+=t.transType==='Buy'?(t.principal||0):0;a.sell+=t.transType==='Sell'?(t.principal||0):0;
if(!a.first||t.tradeDate<a.first)a.first=t.tradeDate;if(t.tradeDate>a.last)a.last=t.tradeDate});
return Object.values(m).map(a=>{a.funds=[...a.cusipSet];delete a.cusipSet;const lastinfo=(load('cpd_informed').find(x=>x.account===a.account)||{});a.lastInformed=lastinfo.date||'';a.informedBy=lastinfo.by||'';
const base=a.lastInformed||a.last||REPORTDATE;a.days=daysBetween(base,TODAY);
a.band=a.days>=90?'red':(a.days>=30?'amber':'green');a.baseDate=base;return a});
}
function reminderSummary(rows){return {red:rows.filter(r=>r.band==='red').length,amber:rows.filter(r=>r.band==='amber').length,green:rows.filter(r=>r.band==='green').length}}
function reminderTable(rows,showAdviser){return table(['Account','Adviser','Company','Last trade','Last informed','Days since update','Traffic light','Set update date'],rows.sort((a,b)=>b.days-a.days).map(a=>'<tr><td><span class="account-link" data-profile="'+esc(a.account)+'">'+esc(a.account)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(a.adviser)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.sponsor)+'</td><td>'+d(a.last)+'</td><td>'+d(a.lastInformed)+'</td><td class="num">'+a.days+'</td><td><span class="pill" style="background:'+(a.band==='red'?'#fde8e8;color:#a3272d':a.band==='amber'?'#fdf3e0;color:#9a6408':'#e6f6ee;color:#146c47')+'">'+(a.band==='red'?'Overdue':a.band==='amber'?'Due soon':'Up to date')+'</span></td><td><input type="date" data-informed="'+esc(a.account)+'" value="'+a.lastInformed+'"></td></tr>'))}
function reminders(){
const rows=accountStats();const s=reminderSummary(rows);
const legend='<div class="grid"><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Overdue (90+ days)</div><div class="value" style="color:var(--red)">'+s.red+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Due soon (30-89 days)</div><div class="value" style="color:var(--amber)">'+s.amber+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Up to date (under 30 days)</div><div class="value" style="color:var(--green)">'+s.green+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Total accounts</div><div class="value">'+rows.length+'</div></div></div>';
const overdue=rows.filter(r=>r.band==='red');
const alerts='<div class="card section"><h2>Reminders needing action</h2>'+(overdue.length?overdue.sort((a,b)=>b.days-a.days).map(r=>'<div class="notice" style="margin-bottom:8px"><b>'+esc(r.account)+'</b> - no investment update recorded for '+r.days+' days. Adviser '+esc(r.adviser)+'.</div>').join(''):'<p class="muted">No overdue accounts under the current 90-day rule.</p>')+'</div>';
const rules='<div class="card section"><h2>Traffic light rules</h2><div class="kpi-list"><div class="kv"><span><span class="pill" style="background:#fde8e8;color:#a3272d">Overdue</span></span><b>90 or more days since last recorded update</b></div><div class="kv"><span><span class="pill" style="background:#fdf3e0;color:#9a6408">Due soon</span></span><b>30 to 89 days</b></div><div class="kv"><span><span class="pill" style="background:#e6f6ee;color:#146c47">Up to date</span></span><b>Under 30 days</b></div></div><p class="muted">Days are counted from the last recorded update date. Where no update has been recorded, the last historical trade date is used as the starting point, so these figures should be replaced with real contact dates by your team.</p></div>';
return view('reminders',legend+'<div class="section card"><h2>Record that a client was informed</h2><form class="form" data-form="cpd_informed"><input name="account" placeholder="Account" required><input name="date" type="date" required><input name="by" placeholder="Adviser or staff member"><input name="channel" placeholder="Call, email or meeting"><button class="primary" type="submit">Save update record</button></form></div>'+alerts+'<div class="section">'+reminderTable(rows,true)+'</div>'+rules+'<div class="section">'+table(['Account','Update recorded','Recorded by','Channel'],load('cpd_informed').map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.account)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.date)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.by||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.channel||'')+'</td></tr>'))+'</div>')}
function clientinfo(){
const rows=accountStats();const term=(state.q||'');
const show=rows.filter(r=>!term||JSON.stringify(r).toLowerCase().includes(term));
const totals=show.reduce((s,r)=>({buy:s.buy+r.buy,sell:s.sell+r.sell,trades:s.trades+r.trades}),{buy:0,sell:0,trades:0});
const summary='<div class="grid"><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Accounts shown</div><div class="value">'+show.length+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Total buy principal</div><div class="value">'+money(totals.buy)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Total sell principal</div><div class="value">'+money(totals.sell)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Trades shown</div><div class="value">'+qty(totals.trades)+'</div></div></div>';
const rowsHtml=show.sort((a,b)=>b.trades-a.trades).map(a=>'<tr><td><span class="account-link" data-profile="'+esc(a.account)+'">'+esc(a.account)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(a.adviser)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.sponsor)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.firm)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.branchName)+'</td><td class="num">'+a.funds.length+'</td><td class="num">'+a.trades+'</td><td class="num">'+money(a.buy)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(a.sell)+'</td><td>'+d(a.first)+'</td><td>'+d(a.last)+'</td><td>'+d(a.informedBy||a.lastInformed||'')+'</td></tr>');
const adviserTotals=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>r.adviser))].filter(Boolean).map(a=>{const g=rows.filter(r=>r.adviser===a);return '<tr><td>'+esc(a)+'</td><td class="num">'+g.length+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(g.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.trades,0))+'</td><td class="num">'+money(g.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.buy,0))+'</td><td class="num">'+money(g.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.sell,0))+'</td></tr>'});
return view('clientinfo',summary+'<div class="section"><h2>Complete account data</h2>'+table(['Account','Adviser / person','Sponsor','Firm','Branch','Funds','Trades','Buy principal','Sell principal','First trade','Last trade','Last informed'],rowsHtml)+'</div><div class="section"><h2>Adviser totals</h2>'+table(['Adviser','Accounts','Trades','Buy principal','Sell principal'],adviserTotals)+'</div>')}

function tradeMove(t){
const px=movePrices[t.cusip]; if(!px) return null;
const tp=t.quantity?t.principal/t.quantity:null; if(!tp) return null;
return {tradePrice:tp,currentPrice:px.unitValue,priceAsOf:px.asOf,movePct:(px.unitValue-tp)/tp,markedValue:t.quantitypx.unitValue,unrealized:t.quantity(px.unitValue-tp)};
}
function accountMoves(account){
const list=D.trades.filter(t=>t.account===account).map(t=>{const m=tradeMove(t);return m?{...t,...m}:{...t,tradePrice:t.quantity?t.principal/t.quantity:null,currentPrice:null,movePct:null,markedValue:null,unrealized:null}});
const priced=list.filter(x=>x.movePct!==null);
const buys=priced.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy');
const invested=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0),marked=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.markedValue,0);
const sells=priced.filter(x=>x.transType==='Sell');
const sellPrincipal=sells.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0),sellMarked=sells.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.markedValue,0);
return {list,priced,buys,sells,invested,marked,buysMovePct:invested?(marked-invested)/invested:null,buysUnrealized:marked-invested,sellPrincipal,sellMarked,sellsCounterfactualPct:sellPrincipal?(sellPrincipal-sellMarked)/sellPrincipal:null,unrealized:priced.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x.transType==='Buy'?x.unrealized:-x.unrealized),0)};
}
function pnlPage(){
const rows=accountStats();const pricedAccounts=rows.filter(r=>accountMoves(r.account).priced.length);
const builds=rows.filter(r=>accountMoves(r.account).priced.length);
const totalInvested=builds.reduce((s,r)=>s+accountMoves(r.account).invested,0);
const totalMarked=builds.reduce((s,r)=>s+accountMoves(r.account).marked,0);
const pricedTrades=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+accountMoves(r.account).priced.length,0);
const unpriced=D.trades.filter(t=>!movePrices[t.cusip]).length;
const pct=totalInvested?(totalMarked-totalInvested)/totalInvested:0;
const head='<div class="grid"><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Price movement on priced buys</div><div class="value">'+(pct>=0?'+':'')+(pct100).toFixed(2)+'%</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Total cost of priced buys</div><div class="value">'+money(totalInvested)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Marked value at 18 Sep 2026</div><div class="value">'+money(totalMarked)+'</div></div><div class="card metric"><div class="muted">Price movement amount</div><div class="value">'+money(totalMarked-totalInvested)+'</div></div></div>';
const caution='<div class="notice section">This is price movement on historical purchases, calculated as current unit value minus the average price paid, divided by the average price paid. It is NOT accounting profit and loss. It ignores fees, FX, distributions, corporate actions, transfers and share disposals, and the current unit value is a fund-level price rather than an account-specific holding value. '+(unpriced?unpriced+' trade(s) could not be priced.':'')+'</div>';
const top=builds.map(r=>({r,m:accountMoves(r.account)})).sort((a,b)=>b.m.invested-a.m.invested);
const tRows=top.map(o=>'<tr><td><span class="account-link" data-profile="'+esc(o.r.account)+'">'+esc(o.r.account)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(o.r.adviser)+'</td><td class="num">'+o.m.priced.length+'</td><td class="num">'+o.m.buys.length+'</td><td class="num">'+o.m.sells.length+'</td><td class="num">'+money(o.m.invested)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(o.m.marked)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(o.m.buysUnrealized)+'</td><td class="num" style="color:'+(o.m.buysMovePct>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)')+'">'+(o.m.buysMovePct>=0?'+':'')+(o.m.buysMovePct100).toFixed(2)+'%</td></tr>');
const secRows=Object.entries(movePrices).sort((a,b)=>a[1].fundName.localeCompare(b[1].fundName)).map(([c,p])=>{
const t=D.trades.filter(x=>x.cusip===c);const priced=t.filter(x=>tradeMove(x));
const buys=priced.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy');const inv=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0),mk=buys.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.markedValue,0);
return '<tr><td>'+esc(c)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.fundName)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(p.unitValue)+'</td><td class="num">'+priced.length+'</td><td class="num">'+(inv?((mk-inv)/inv100).toFixed(2)+'%':'')+'</td></tr>'});
return view('pnl',head+caution+'<div class="section"><h2>Price movement by account ('+top.length+' accounts with priced trades, largest cost first)</h2>'+table(['Account','Adviser','Priced trades','Buys','Sells','Cost of buys','Marked value','Movement','Movement %'],tRows)+'</div><div class="section"><h2>Current unit values used</h2><p class="muted">All marked values use the 18 Sep 2026 unit value for the matched fund. Only '+Object.keys(movePrices).length+' securities have a supplied price.</p>'+table(['CUSIP','Fund','Current unit value','Priced trades','Movement %'],secRows)+'</div>')}
function clients(){const rows=accountRows().map(a=>'<tr><td><span class="account-link" data-account="'+esc(a.account)+'">'+esc(a.account)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(a.sponsor)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.branch)+'</td><td>'+esc(a.adviser)+'</td><td class="num">'+a.trades+'</td><td class="num">'+money(a.buy)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(a.sell)+'</td><td>'+d(a.last)+'</td></tr>');return view('clients','<div class="controls"><span class="pill">'+rows.length+' accounts. Identifiers are from the trade report; verified client names were not provided.</span></div>'+table(['Account','Sponsor','Branch','Adviser','Trades','Buy principal','Sell principal','Last trade'],rows))}
function funds(){return view('funds',table(['CUSIP','Fund','Style','Positions','Quantity','Observed value','Unit value'],fundRows().map(f=>'<tr><td><span class="account-link" data-fund="'+esc(f.cusip)+'">'+esc(f.cusip)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(f.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(f.style)+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(f.positions)+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(f.quantity)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(f.value)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(f.unit)+'</td></tr>')))}
function trades(){const controls='<div class="controls"><select id="accountFilter"><option value="">All accounts</option>'+[...new Set(D.trades.map(x=>x.account))].sort().map(x=>'<option '+(state.account===x?'selected':'')+'>'+esc(x)+'</option>').join('')+'</select><select id="typeFilter"><option value="">Buy and sell</option><option '+(state.type==='Buy'?'selected':'')+'>Buy</option><option '+(state.type==='Sell'?'selected':'')+'>Sell</option></select><input id="from" type="date" value="'+state.from+'"><input id="to" type="date" value="'+state.to+'"><button class="primary" id="clearFilters">Clear</button><span class="pill">'+filteredTrades().length+' records</span></div>';const rows=filteredTrades().sort((a,b)=>b.tradeDate.localeCompare(a.tradeDate)).map(t=>'<tr><td>'+d(t.tradeDate)+'</td><td><span class="account-link" data-account="'+esc(t.account)+'">'+esc(t.account)+'</span></td><td>'+esc(t.cusip)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.product)+'</td><td class="'+(t.transType==='Buy'?'buy':'sell')+'">'+t.transType+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(t.quantity)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(t.principal)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(t.quantity?t.principal/t.quantity:null)+'</td><td>'+esc(t.programType)+'</td></tr>');return view('trades',controls+table(['Date','Account','CUSIP','Product','Type','Quantity','Principal','Implied unit price','Program'],rows))}
function positions(){const rows=D.positions.filter(p=>!state.q||JSON.stringify(p).toLowerCase().includes(state.q)).sort((a,b)=>b.totalValue-a.totalValue).map(p=>'<tr><td>'+d(p.asOf)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.sponsorId)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.sponsorName)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.cusip)+'</td><td>'+esc(p.fundName)+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(p.positions)+'</td><td class="num">'+qty(p.totalQuantity)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(p.totalValue)+'</td><td class="num">'+money(p.unitValue)+'</td></tr>');return view('positions','<div class="notice">This report is aggregated by sponsor and fund. It is not an account-level current-holdings report.</div><div class="section">'+table(['As of','Sponsor ID','Sponsor','CUSIP','Fund','Positions','Quantity','Value','Unit value'],rows)+'</div>')}
function editable(page,key,fields){const data=load(key);const inputs=fields.map(f=>f[1]==='textarea'?'<textarea name="'+f[0]+'" placeholder="'+f[2]+'"></textarea>':'<input name="'+f[0]+'" type="'+f[1]+'" placeholder="'+f[2]+'" required>').join('');const rows=data.map((x,i)=>'<tr>'+fields.map(f=>'<td>'+esc(x[f[0]])+'</td>').join('')+'<td><button class="danger" data-del="'+key+'|'+i+'">Delete</button></td></tr>');return view(page,'<div class="card"><form class="form" data-form="'+key+'">'+inputs+'<button class="primary" type="submit">Save</button></form></div><div class="section">'+table(fields.map(f=>f[2]).concat(''),rows)+'</div>')}
function controls(){return view('import','<div class="card"><h2>Data loaded</h2><div class="kpi-list"><div class="kv"><span>Trade records extracted</span><b>'+D.trades.length+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Position rows extracted</span><b>'+D.positions.length+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Accounts</span><b>'+accountRows().length+'</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Report date</span><b>'+d(D.reportAsOf)+'</b></div></div></div><div class="card section"><h2>Production controls</h2><p>This is a self-contained historical-data prototype. It runs locally in the browser and saves tasks, meetings and notes in this browser only.</p><div class="notice">Before using it for confidential client information, deploy it behind authenticated access with MFA, role permissions, encrypted storage, backups and an audit log. A static HTML file is not a secure production database.</div><p><b>Still needed for complete client portfolios:</b> verified client names, account-level current positions, approved cost basis, fees, distributions, FX rules, emails and contact history.</p></div>')}
function accountDetail(account){const ts=D.trades.filter(t=>t.account===account).sort((a,b)=>b.tradeDate.localeCompare(a.tradeDate));const buy=ts.filter(x=>x.transType==='Buy').reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0),sell=ts.filter(x=>x.transType==='Sell').reduce((s,x)=>s+x.principal,0);state.page='trades';state.account=account;render();setTimeout(()=>{},0)}
function render(){nav();$('#title').textContent=pages.find(x=>x[0]===state.page)?.[1]||'Client Portfolio Desk';$('#views').innerHTML=dashboard()+clientDesk()+clientinfo()+reminders()+pnlPage()+clients()+funds()+trades()+positions()+editable('tasks','cpd_tasks',[['account','text','Account'],['task','text','Task'],['owner','text','Owner'],['due','date','Due date'],['status','text','Status']])+editable('meetings','cpd_meetings',[['account','text','Account'],['date','date','Date'],['attendees','text','Attendees'],['summary','textarea','Meeting summary'],['followup','text','Follow-up']])+editable('notes','cpd_notes',[['account','text','Account'],['timestamp','datetime-local','Timestamp'],['author','text','Added by'],['note','textarea','Note']])+controls();
const ps=$('#profileSearch'),pb=$('#profileSearchBtn');if(ps){const go=()=>{state.profileQ=ps.value;state.profileKey='';render()};ps.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')go()};if(pb)pb.onclick=go}
document.querySelectorAll('[data-profile]').forEach(e=>e.onclick=()=>{state.profileKey=e.dataset.profile;render()});
const bk=$('#backToDesk');if(bk)bk.onclick=()=>{state.profileKey='';render()};

const inf=document.querySelectorAll('[data-informed]');inf.forEach(i=>i.onchange=()=>{const a=load('cpd_informed').filter(x=>x.account!==i.dataset.informed);a.push({account:i.dataset.informed,date:i.value,by:'',channel:''});save('cpd_informed',a);render()});
document.querySelectorAll('[data-account]').forEach(e=>e.onclick=()=>{state.account=e.dataset.account;state.page='trades';render()});document.querySelectorAll('[data-fund]').forEach(e=>e.onclick=()=>{state.fund=e.dataset.fund;state.page='trades';render()});
const af=$('#accountFilter');if(af)af.onchange=e=>{state.account=e.target.value;render()};const tf=$('#typeFilter');if(tf)tf.onchange=e=>{state.type=e.target.value;render()};const fr=$('#from');if(fr)fr.onchange=e=>{state.from=e.target.value;render()};const to=$('#to');if(to)to.onchange=e=>{state.to=e.target.value;render()};const cl=$('#clearFilters');if(cl)cl.onclick=()=>{state.account=state.fund=state.type=state.from=state.to='';render()};
document.querySelectorAll('[data-form]').forEach(f=>f.onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();const key=f.dataset.form,o=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(f));const a=load(key);a.push(o);save(key,a);render()});document.querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const [k,i]=b.dataset.del.split('|'),a=load(k);a.splice(Number(i),1);save(k,a);render()});}
$('#globalSearch').oninput=e=>{state.q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render();$('#globalSearch').value=e.target.value;$('#globalSearch').focus()};render();
</script></body></html>
